}
function pend(c,t,x,y,L,a,showArc=true,baseline=null){
  if(showArc){
   c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(255,229,181,.57)';c.lineWidth=2;c.setLineDash([5,8]);c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,L,Math.PI/2-a,Math.PI/2+a);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);c.restore();
  }
  if(baseline!==null){c.save();c.strokeStyle='#b3d7ec';c.setLineDash([5,6]);c.lineWidth=2;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,L,Math.PI/2-baseline,Math.PI/2+baseline);c.stroke();c.restore();}
  const px=x+L*Math.sin(a*Math.cos(t)),py=y+L*Math.cos(a*Math.cos(t));
  c.save();c.shadowColor='#041524';c.shadowBlur=13;c.shadowOffsetX=4;c.shadowOffsetY=5;c.strokeStyle='#1d2b31';c.lineWidth=11;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(px,py);c.stroke();c.restore();
  const rg=c.createLinearGradient(x-6,y,px+4,py);rg.addColorStop(0,'#fff6dc');rg.addColorStop(.30,'#e1c991');rg.addColorStop(.55,'#6f7980');rg.addColorStop(1,'#f3d89b');
  c.strokeStyle=rg;c.lineWidth=6;c.lineCap='round';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(px,py);c.stroke();
  bloom(c,px,py,65,'#fff1cf',.18);
  c.save();c.shadowColor='#0a1723';c.shadowBlur=23;c.shadowOffsetY=13;
  const metal=c.createRadialGradient(px-13,py-14,1,px,py,41);metal.addColorStop(0,'#fff9e2');metal.addColorStop(.26,'#ddcaac');metal.addColorStop(.57,'#74848b');metal.addColorStop(.83,'#313b45');metal.addColorStop(1,'#111f2c');
  disc(c,px,py,32,metal,'#f7e7cb',2);c.restore();
  c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.9)';c.lineWidth=3;c.beginPath();c.arc(px-2,py,26,3.35,4.68);c.stroke();c.restore();
  disc(c,x,y,15,'#253c46','#e8c990',4);disc(c,x,y,5,'#fff1cd');
  return [px,py];
}
function movement(c,t,x,y,lit){
  // Moving ring and pulse around a separate driven transmission gear.
  c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.rotate(t);
  c.shadowColor=lit?'#ffe0a0':'#142f3e';c.shadowBlur=lit?19:6;
  const edge=c.createLinearGradient(-42,-30,42,35);edge.addColorStop(0,'#ffe7ae');edge.addColorStop(.4,'#8f7044');edge.addColorStop(.9,'#dfb67b');
  disc(c,0,0,35,edge,'#fff0cf',2.5);
  disc(c,0,0,27,'#193747','#b89862',4);
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/4;c.lineWidth=6;c.strokeStyle='#dfb87e';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*8,Math.sin(a)*8);c.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*26,Math.sin(a)*26);c.stroke()}
  disc(c,0,0,8,'#e7c895','#fff3cb',2);c.restore();
  if(lit){bloom(c,x,y,91,'#d3ffe6',.5);disc(c,x,y+61,4,'#c1ffe0')}
}
function bridge(c,n){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=51+i*158,done=i<n;round(c,x,341+(done?0:15),144,27,5,done?'#b9a57b':'#243849',done?'#ffe6b4':'#8d9fab',2);for(const xx of [x+12,x+131])disc(c,xx,355+(done?0:15),3,done?'#fff4de':'#6f8999');}}
function tower(c,t,kind){
  // Maintain the cinematic frame aspect ratio on desktop; never stretch physics drawings.
  if(c.canvas.width>=700 && c.canvas.height < c.canvas.width*0.80) c.canvas.height=Math.round(c.canvas.width*470/560);
  setup(c);
  const boss=kind==='boss',resonance=kind==='rhythm',bb=boss?clockBoss:null;
  const T=boss?period(bb.length,bb.angle):resonance?period(60,12):period(state.bridgeLength,10);
  const phaseT=(boss&&bb.phase===1)?t-bb.startTime:(kind==='bridge'&&state.bridgeReady)?t-state.bridgeStart:t;
  const a=resonance?(6+4*Math.min(4,state.rhythmHit))*Math.PI/180:boss?bb.angle*Math.PI/180:.35;
  const phase=2*Math.PI*phaseT/T;const lit=resonance?state.rhythmHit>0:boss?bb.phase===2||t-(bb.gearPulseTime??-100)<1:state.bridgeHits>0;
  const warm=(Math.sin(t*.7)*.5+.5);
  bloom(c,530,207,180,'#ffbd67',.12+.13*warm);
  head(c,boss?'FINAL MISSION · 時計塔':resonance?'DISCOVERY · 共振':'ACTION · 振り子',boss?'時計塔の最終修理':resonance?'共振ブランコ':'2秒のリズム橋');
  if(resonance){
   const deg=6+Math.min(4,state.rhythmHit)*4,rad=deg*Math.PI/180;
   pend(c,phase,211,121,209,rad,true,6*Math.PI/180);
   round(c,341,124,173,104,13,'rgba(4,22,33,.85)','#f9d494',1.5);
   txt(c,'振れ幅の変化',355,144,16,'#ffe0a0');txt(c,'最初  6°',355,175,17,'#d5e4e9');txt(c,`いま  ${deg}°`,355,205,23,'#acffe1');
   const zone=Math.abs(Math.sin(phase))>.89;
   if(zone)bloom(c,211,330,90,'#b1ffe0',.52);
   foot(c,`タイミング ${state.rhythmHit}/4`,zone?'● 中央通過！ 押すタイミング':'● 振れ幅の増加を見つけよう',zone);
  }else{
   pend(c,phase,175,114,boss ? 220 : (145+(state.bridgeLength-60)*.85),a,true,null);
   const age=boss?t-(bb.gearPulseTime??-100):t-state.bridgePulseTime;
   movement(c,(boss?(bb.phase===2?t*.52:bb.hits*.68):state.bridgeHits*.66)+Math.max(0,Math.min(1,age))*.4,449,199,lit);
   if(!boss)bridge(c,state.bridgeHits);
   if(boss){
    txt(c,'動力：ぜんまい',353,281,16,'#ffdfaa');
    foot(c,bb.phase===2?'時計塔が復活！':bb.phase===0?'1秒振り子を作ろう':`時計塔の修理 ${bb.hits}/3`,bb.phase===2?'歯車が振り子の周期に合わせて進む':bb.phase===0?'10往復から周期を調べよう':'中央でタイミングよくスイッチを押そう',bb.phase===2);
   }else{
    const center=state.bridgeReady&&Math.abs(Math.sin(phase))>.75;
    if(center)bloom(c,175,114+(145+(state.bridgeLength-60)*.85),80,'#aaffdf',.6);
    foot(c,`橋の修理 ${state.bridgeHits}/3`,state.bridgeReady?(center?'● いま！ 中央を通過':'● 真下に来るタイミングを待つ'):'① 糸の長さを調整して周期を測る',center);
   }
  }
  c.restore();
}
mobileBridge=function(c,t){if(!loaded())return olderBridge(c,t);tower(c,t,'bridge')};
mobileRhythm=function(c,t,v){if(!loaded())return olderRhythm(c,t,v);tower(c,t,'rhythm')};
mobileBossClock=function(c,t){if(!loaded())return olderBoss($('#bossClockCanvas'),t);tower(c,t,'boss')};
// Existing rendering uses wide canvas on desktop. Reuse the cinematic scene instead of reverting to the old diagram.
drawScene=function(canvas,t,s){if(loaded()&&s.mode==='beatbridge'){tower(canvas.getContext('2d'),t,'bridge');return;}return olderScene(canvas,t,s)};
const prevPendulum=drawPendulum;

drawBossClock=function(canvas,t){if(canvas&&loaded()){tower(canvas.getContext('2d'),t,'boss');return;}return olderBoss(canvas,t)};

<script id="immersive-hud-v018">
(()=>{
 'use strict';
 // Dynamic legacy theme styles mount during game initialization; keep immersive overrides last.
 document.head.appendChild(document.getElementById('fullbleed-v018'));
 // Add a clear adventure launch on the full-bleed title image.
 const heroText=document.querySelector('.hero.chronicle-hero .hero-text');
 if(heroText){const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='hero-start-row';row.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="hero-start" id="heroStartGame">▶ 冒険を始める <small>次のミッションへ</small></button><button type="button" class="hero-substart" id="heroSeeMap">🗺️ マップを見る</button>';heroText.append(row);
 row.querySelector('#heroStartGame').onclick=()=>{const recommended=document.querySelector('#goRecommended');if(recommended){recommended.click();}else{document.querySelector('.tab[data-view="world"]')?.click();}};
 row.querySelector('#heroSeeMap').onclick=()=>document.querySelector('.story-hero')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
 }
 const names={map:['🗺️','冒険'],world:['⚙️','探索'],boss:['⚔️','ボス'],lab:['🔬','実験'],quiz:['📖','知識'],teacher:['📋','先生']};
 document.querySelectorAll('.tabs .tab').forEach(btn=>{const x=names[btn.dataset.view];if(x){btn.setAttribute('title',btn.textContent.trim());btn.innerHTML='<span class="dock-icon" aria-hidden="true">'+x[0]+'</span><span class="dock-word">'+x[1]+'</span>';}});
 let drawerOpen=false;let commandOpen=false;
 const app=document.querySelector('#app');
 function sync(){
  const current=document.querySelector('.tab[aria-current="page"]');
  const scene=current?.dataset.view||'map';document.body.dataset.scene=scene;
  const columns=app.querySelector('.columns');
  if(scene!=='world'||!columns){document.body.classList.remove('stage-drawer-open');drawerOpen=false;commandOpen=false;return;}
  const aside=columns.querySelector(':scope > aside.panel');
  const section=columns.querySelector(':scope > section.panel');
  if(!aside||!section)return;
  aside.classList.add('stage-list-drawer');
  if(section.querySelector('.stage-viewport'))return;
  const pad=section.querySelector(':scope > .panelpad');if(!pad)return;
  const viewport=document.createElement('div');viewport.className='stage-viewport';
  const top=document.createElement('div');top.className='stage-hudtop';
  const stage=document.createElement('div');stage.className='stage-scene';
  const commands=document.createElement('div');commands.className='stage-command-panel';commands.setAttribute('aria-label','科学装置の操作パネル');
  const other=document.createElement('div');other.className='world-after';
  for(const c of [...pad.children]){
   if(c.matches('.section-kicker,.challenge-ribbon,.headline,.brief,.mobile-select'))top.append(c);
   else if(c.matches('.focus-console,.scene-label,.visual-frame'))stage.append(c);
   else if(c.matches('.controls'))commands.append(c);
   else other.append(c);
  }
  const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='stage-floatbar';bar.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="game-float" data-hud-action="stages" aria-expanded="false">☷ ステージ</button><button type="button" class="game-float main-command" data-hud-action="controls" aria-expanded="false">⚙ しかけを操作</button><button type="button" class="game-float" data-hud-action="observe">👁 観察</button>';
  const back=document.createElement('button');back.type='button';back.className='floating-gameback';back.textContent='↩ 冒険マップ';back.dataset.hudAction='map';
  const label=document.createElement('div');label.className='stage-sheet-top';label.innerHTML='<strong>⚙ SCIENCE COMMAND</strong><button type="button" class="close-sheet" data-hud-action="close">✕ 閉じる</button>';commands.prepend(label);
  viewport.append(top,stage,commands,bar,back);pad.append(viewport,other);
  if(commandOpen)viewport.classList.add('commands-open');
  if(drawerOpen)document.body.classList.add('stage-drawer-open');
 }
 function setPanel(open){commandOpen=!!open;const vp=app.querySelector('.stage-viewport');if(vp)vp.classList.toggle('commands-open',commandOpen);const btn=app.querySelector('[data-hud-action="controls"]');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(commandOpen));btn.textContent=commandOpen?'✕ 操作を閉じる':'⚙ しかけを操作';}}
 app.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-hud-action]');if(!b)return;
  const action=b.dataset.hudAction;
  if(action==='controls')setPanel(!commandOpen);
  else if(action==='close'||action==='observe')setPanel(false);
  else if(action==='stages'){drawerOpen=!drawerOpen;document.body.classList.toggle('stage-drawer-open',drawerOpen);b.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(drawerOpen));}
  else if(action==='map'){document.querySelector('.tab[data-view="map"]')?.click();}
 });
 app.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('[data-stage]')){drawerOpen=false;document.body.classList.remove('stage-drawer-open');}});
 let scheduled=false;
 const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;sync();});});
 observer.observe(app,{childList:true});
 document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{commandOpen=false;drawerOpen=false;queueMicrotask(sync);}));
 sync();
})();
</script>
